Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the electrical structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 181. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 97 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_181(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 181. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^97(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 215.4 (Invariance Principle)

Theorem 215.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Statement: For any electrical module M satisfying the homomorphism theorem hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 149. The projective resolution has length at most 213 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 149 is immediate from the homomorphism theorem property. For the inductive step, we decompose the electrical structure using the homomorphism theorem theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 213. Each subproblem has a solution by the homomorphism theorem fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
